FKTEN - THENNARASAN A/L SABAPATHY
FKTEN - TIJJANI ADAM
FKTEN - VIJAYASARVESWARI A/P VEERAPERUMAL
FKTEN - VIKNESWARAN A/L VIJEAN
FKTEN - WAN AZLIANAWATI BT WAN AZIZ
FKTEN - WAN MOKHDZANI BIN WAN NOR HAIMI
FKTEN - WEE FWEN HOON
FKTEN - YUFRIDIN BIN WAHAB
FKTEN - ZALIMAN BIN SAULI
FKTEN - ZARIMAWATY BINTI ZAILAN
FKTEN - ZULKARNAY BIN ZAKARIA
INEE - JASNI BIN MOHAMED ISMAIL
INEE - LIU WEI WEN
INEE - MOHAMAD FARIS BIN MOHAMAD FATHIL
INEE - MOHAMMAD ISA BIN AHMAD AZAN
INEE - MOHAMMAD NUZAIHAN BIN MD NOR
INEE - NOR AZIZAH BINTI PARMIN
INEE - NUR HAMIDAH BINTI ABDUL HALIM
INEE - RAMZAN BIN MAT AYUB
INEE - RUSLINDA BINTI A. RAHIM
INEE - UDA BIN HASHIM
INEE - VOON CHUN HONG
Must come earlier:
FKTEN - MOHD NAZRI BIN A. KARIM
FKTEN - MUHAMAD HAFIZ BIN AB AZIZ
FKTEN - NORFATIHAH BINTI BAHARI
Must come much earlier:
FKTEN - AHMAD ASHRAF BIN ABDUL HALIM
FKTEN - 'AINI SYUHADA BINTI MD ZAIN
FKTEN - LIYANA BINTI ZAHID
FKTEN - MOHD HAFIZUDDIN BIN MAT
FKTEN - MUHAMMAD SOLIHIN BIN ZULKEFLI
FKTEN - NOR ROSHIDAH BINTI YUSOF
FKTEN - NUR HIDAYAH BINTI RAMLI
FKTEN - WEE FWEN HOON
Free day:
FKTEN - ABDUL HAFIIZH BIN ISMAIL
FKTEN - AFZAN BINTI KAMARUDIN
FKTEN - AHMAD FARIZ BIN HASAN
FKTEN - AHMAD HUSNI BIN MOHD SHAPRI
FKTEN - AHMAD NASIR BIN CHE ROSLI
FKTEN - AHMAD NASRUL BIN NORALI
FKTEN - AHMAD SYAHIR BIN AHMAD BAKHIT
FKTEN - AMIR NAZREN BIN ABDUL RAHIM
FKTEN - ANAS BIN MOHD NOOR

FKTEN - ARIF MAWARDI BIN ISMAIL @ SUHAIMEE
FKTEN - ASRAL BIN BAHARI JAMBEK
FKTEN - ASYRAF HAKIMI BIN ABU BAKAR
